- **product-spec-generator**: This prompt instructs the AI to produce a structured feature specification document for a proposed product feature, using provided inputs such as target users, key functionalities, technical considerations, and estimated development effort. The resulting output is a clear, implementation-ready spec organized into standard product and engineering sections—overview, goals with success metrics, user stories, acceptance criteria mapped to those stories, a high-level technical design, and future considerations—so developers can begin building with aligned requirements and scope. - **product-specification**: Turn ambiguous product ideas into clear product requirements, user stories, acceptance criteria, scope boundaries, and delivery plans. Use for feature discovery, MVP definition, stakeholder alignment, backlog refinement, and product change proposals. ([mhingan/agent-forge](https://github.com/mhingan/agent-forge/tree/HEAD/skills/product-specification) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **product-specification**: Create or review a concise, user- and product-focused feature specification that strictly follows the supplied template. Describe user stories, journeys, and observable experiences without cabinet deliberations or implementation details. Use when defining a feature, writing a feature spec or PRD, clarifying scope, or assessing specification completeness. ([willtsai/agentic-skills](https://github.com/willtsai/agentic-skills/tree/HEAD/product-cabinet/skills/product-specification) · ★1)
- **product-specification**: Use this skill when planning or changing product behavior in Clash Command. ([AmruthLP12/chronix_flutter](https://github.com/AmruthLP12/chronix_flutter/tree/HEAD/.agent/product-specification) · 3 copies)
- **product-specification**: Draft, change, or review specifications and sequence an implementation roadmap with a human before coding. Use for requirements discovery, architecture decisions, acceptance criteria, requirement drift and implementation readiness. Not mandatory for routine fixes or fully specified small changes. ([NanoNative/nano_railix](https://github.com/NanoNative/nano_railix/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/product-specification))
- **product-specification**: Guide a non-technical client from a rough idea to a concise, detailed, non-technical product specification in chat markdown. ([setaman/inspectr](https://github.com/setaman/inspectr/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/product-specification))
- **product-specification-engineering**: Compile researched user goals, accepted decisions, concept relations and qualified reusable technology into traceable, complete product and architecture contracts before substantial development. Audit states, variants, interfaces, ownership, permissions, failure paths and acceptance. Skip routine changes with settled contracts. ([GBIDEVELOPMENT/general-purpose](https://github.com/GBIDEVELOPMENT/general-purpose/tree/HEAD/skills/product-specification-engineering))
- **product-specifications**: Convert customer needs into measurable target specifications and later final specifications for any product, service, or project, with metrics, units, ideal and target values, competitive benchmarks, and a needs-metrics matrix. Use this skill whenever the user mentions specifications, specs, requirements, metrics, engineering targets, acceptance criteria, quality function deployment, or House of Quality, or needs to make "good" measurable, even if they don't use these terms. ([Hem456anth/pea](https://github.com/Hem456anth/pea/tree/HEAD/skills/product-specifications))
- **product-specifier**: Use when turning a greenfield product idea into an unambiguous, ID-indexed specs/PRD.md with functional stories, Gherkin acceptance criteria, priorities, non-functional budgets, and AI/LLM guardrails. ([nizaalkhot/skills](https://github.com/nizaalkhot/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/product-specifier))
- **product-specify**: Use when you want guided trade-off analysis, multi-option comparison, or structured product decision facilitation before documenting. Class boots only suggest capture — they never write drafts; running this skill writes the lightweight PDR draft. ([tikalk/adlc-team-skills](https://github.com/tikalk/adlc-team-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/product/product-specify) · ★145)
- **product-specify**: Interactive PRD exploration and Product Decision Record creation (greenfield) ([chaimt/agentic_engineer_course](https://github.com/chaimt/agentic_engineer_course/tree/HEAD/presentation/.claude/skills/product-specify) · 3 copies)
- **product-specs**: Generates and refines detailed Product Requirement Documents (PRDs) for epics and tasks defined in specs/&lt;issue-number&gt;/roadmap.md. Strictly focuses on product requirements, user journeys, and UX behaviors without proposing technical solutions, class names, or database schemas. Consults advisor agents to formulate product/UX open questions with selectable checkbox options, driving an iterative reconciliation loop until confidence exceeds 90%. ([sonzra/sonzra](https://github.com/sonzra/sonzra/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/product-specs) · ★2)
- **product-specs**: Product specification standards for Spec-Driven Development (SDD) in specs/spec.{ID}.md. ([leozizz/dinheirizz](https://github.com/leozizz/dinheirizz/tree/HEAD/.skills/product-specs) · 2 copies)
- **product-specs**: Author, deliver, and verify product specs in the Memory knowledge graph using the product-specs schema — Capability, Requirement, Scenario, Change, Artifact, TaskGroup, Task, Release, TestSuite — instead of keeping openspec/ files in the repo. Use when creating or revising a spec change, listing capabilities or requirements, marking a feature delivered in a release, linking verification, or tracing task→requirement coverage. ([mkucharz/product-specs-blueprint](https://github.com/mkucharz/product-specs-blueprint/tree/HEAD/skills/product-specs))
- **product-specs**: Product specs: PRD, user stories, acceptance criteria (Given/When/Then), edge cases, analytics events, release plan, rollout/rollback. Use for specs. ([SaketSinghRajput/AGENTSHQ](https://github.com/SaketSinghRajput/AGENTSHQ/tree/HEAD/teams/cpo/skills/product-specs))
- **product-specs**: Enforces clear product specifications (PRDs) — leading with the problem and the user, setting goals and non-goals before features, writing prioritized testable requirements as user stories with acceptance criteria, and defining success metrics and open questions. Use when writing or reviewing a product spec, PRD, feature brief, requirements doc, or acceptance criteria — anything describing WHAT to build and WHY before any code.
